| ★ | **Underground Allotment** | Farming | Plant every farmland crop below Y=-50 | 140 |
| · | **Understory** | Farming | Plant a Sapling directly underneath a block of Leaves | 15 |

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Cake Hoarder** — Unchanged this round. Function-granted. bucket_slow fires only when minecraft.used:minecraft.cake has changed. detect/cake_hoarder identifies the chunk by summoning a marker at the block-aligned position (`execute at @s align xz run summon`), reading its Pos - an exact integer, so no assumption has to be made about how `data get`'s scale argument rounds - and floor-dividing by 16 with the scoreboard's /= (which is floorDiv, correct on both sides of the origin). Any change in either chunk coordinate zeroes the running count. The delta is consumed before any early exit so it can never bank up, and a creative placement is thrown away AND ends the run. The count is reset on the grant path.
- **Dig Site** — Function-granted, reusing this pack's Sniffer Sommelier evidence: an ancient seed item lying within five blocks of a Sniffer with no Thrower tag was dug up there, because an item a player dropped carries that player's UUID in Thrower for the rest of its life. detect/dig_site adds one thing to that - positioned at the Sniffer it also reads the block directly beneath it, which is the block being dug - and ticks off moss_block, mud and rooted_dirt separately. If the Sniffer wanders off the dug block before the medium-bucket sample lands, that dig is simply not credited and the player digs again.
- **Farm to Table** — Unchanged this round. Six criteria, all required by the default requirements: three placed_block criteria for the wheat, potato and beetroot crop blocks with location_check pinned to the End, and three consume_item criteria for bread, baked potato and beetroot soup with a `player` entity_properties predicate pinning the eating to the End. Item provenance is untrackable, so planting all three crops in the End plus eating all three foods there is the expressible form of 'a meal made only from End-grown crops'.
- **Greenhouse Gases** — DETECTOR. bacaphd_gg_mined mirrors minecraft.mined:minecraft.wheat and is seeded from each player's current lifetime total in seed.mcfunction before any detector can read it, so the first sample cannot mistake a whole save's wheat harvest for one break. On the tick the mirror moves, the detector runs at the player: five cheap `if block` probes up the column abort immediately unless there is Glass overhead, and only then does the 13x13x5 scan above the player count Glass, granting at 100. The scan is centred on the player rather than on the exact broken block (the criteria layer gives no break position), so a harvest at the extreme edge of a small roof can miss - a roof of 12x12 or better always registers.
- **Groundskeeper Willie** — Five minecraft:item_used_on_block criteria in BACAP's building/colors_of_the_wind shape: a `location` list holding a location_check on the clicked block and a match_tool on the item. Correction to the idea: it listed podzol and mycelium, which Java's hoe tillable table does not contain - only grass_block, dirt and dirt_path (to farmland) and coarse_dirt and rooted_dirt (to dirt) are hoeable. Including them would have made the advancement uncompletable, so the set is the five real ones and the description ('every block a Hoe will change') stays literally true. The six hoes are enumerated rather than using #minecraft:hoes, matching how the rest of this batch avoids unattested tags.
- **Herbivore** — Twenty minecraft.used statistic objectives (every meat, fish, rabbit stew, rotten flesh, spider eye and milk bucket) are summed every 100 ticks; if the sum is unchanged the streak gains 100 ticks, if it moved the streak is wiped, and 240000 ticks (ten in-game days of online time) grants it. The mirror is a private sum objective used by nothing else, and detect/init_b2 parks it at -1 so the very first sweep is guaranteed to be treated as a reset rather than as a lifetime total. Honey, pumpkin pie, cookies and cake count as plant matter here, matching BACAP's own 'Vegetarian' list. Caveat: the ITEM_USED statistic fires when eating begins, so cancelling a bite of steak still breaks the streak. The streak is zeroed on the grant path. Unchanged this round.
- **Meltdown** — minecraft:tick with a player predicate, exactly the shape Enhanced Discoveries adventure/water_dependent uses. `minecraft:type_specific/player.looking_at` with an entity predicate is BACAP's own pattern (monsters/blind_stare), and an `nbt` string inside `looking_at` is attested by Enhanced Discoveries animal/fox_poses; `location.biomes` inside an entity predicate is attested by BACAP monsters/melting. A Snow Golem has 4 max health and takes exactly 1 melt damage per invulnerability cycle in a biome hotter than 1.0, so it passes through Health 3, 2 and 1 for roughly a second each - three criteria in a single OR group give the player about three seconds of window in which to be looking straight at it. requirements is one group so any one of the three grants; the group covers all three criterion names exactly. Caveat I am flagging rather than hiding: the nbt match is exact, so a golem that took some other odd-sized damage first (a fall, a skeleton arrow) would step past 3.0/2.0/1.0 and would not fire - the fix is to bring an undamaged golem.
- **Nest Egg** — minecraft:placed_block with two location_check terms on the same event: the placed block is a Sniffer Egg, and the block one below it is Moss, which is the vanilla rule that halves the hatch time. The offsetY:-1 form is lifted from blazeandcave:biomes/pale_in_comparison. Honest caveat: this fires on the placement, not on the hatch - proving the hatch would need a detector and the placement is what the moss rule is about. I reworded the description from 'Hatch a Sniffer Egg' to 'Plant a Sniffer Egg' so it does not promise more than it checks.
- **Nether Orchard** — Unchanged this round. grown_tree is exactly the mechanism BACAP uses for farming/one_course_meal: item_used_on_block records the clicked position after the use resolves, so when bone meal turns a sapling into a tree the block there is now a log, matched against #minecraft:logs_that_burn, with the dimension pinned to the Nether. Nether stems are outside that tag, so bone-mealing a fungus cannot trigger it. A second required criterion, planted_sapling, makes the description literal - the player must have placed a #minecraft:saplings block in the Nether. Only bone-mealed growth is detectable; a sapling left to grow on its own will not fire.
- **Old Growth** — Pure criteria, no tick cost, and structurally identical to BACAP's farming/one_course_meal: bone meal is matched with match_tool while the location_check reads the block AT the clicked position AFTER the use, so a criterion only fires if the sapling that was standing there has become a log of that species. Four criteria, all required. Stated plainly: Dark Oak and Pale Oak saplings cannot grow at all outside a 2x2, so those two criteria really do prove a giant tree; Spruce and Jungle can also grow as single trees, so those two prove only that bone meal turned a sapling of that species into a tree - the 2x2 trunk itself is not verified, because a location predicate can only see the one block that was clicked. It also inherits one_course_meal's assumption that the trigger reports the block after the growth, which is the assumption BACAP has shipped for years.
- **Sniffer Sommelier** — Function-granted, and rebuilt again this round around the DIG rather than the pickup, because the pickup statistic can never tell a dug seed from a re-picked one. bucket_medium gates on a Sniffer within sixteen blocks of the player - a type-filtered entity lookup with no NBT, cheap enough to run every 0.5 s and rare enough that everything below almost never runs. detect/sniffer_watch then executes `at` that Sniffer and looks for a Torchflower Seed or a Pitcher Pod item entity lying within five blocks of it that has NO Thrower tag. Torchflower Seeds and Pitcher Pods have no vanilla source other than a Sniffer dig, and an item a player throws or drops keeps that player's UUID in Thrower for the rest of its life, so 'seed on the ground at a Sniffer's feet with no Thrower' is the dig itself. Dropping two hoarded seeds next to your Sniffer and picking them back up now proves nothing - both would carry Thrower. Each half stamps its own day number (#today, refreshed every medium tick from `time query day`), and the grant fires when the two stamps are equal; they start at -1 and -2 so an uninitialised player can never match. The bucket line carries gamemode=!spectator, gamemode=!creative and advancements={...=false}. The two pickup statistics and their mirrors are gone entirely.
- **Stable Diet** — Pure criteria, copied structurally from BACAP's animal/live_and_let_dye (item predicate plus an entity condition list holding one minecraft:entity_properties on "this"). Note the interaction only registers when the horse can actually eat - tamed, or hurt, or a foal - because an adult tamed horse at full health returns a consumed interaction while a wild full-health one may not; feeding a horse you have tamed always works.
- **Underground Allotment** — blazeandcave:farming/come_to_the_countryside's exact criterion structure - minecraft:placed_block with a location_check on the placed crop block - with a position.y.max added to each of the same eight crops it uses. placed_block's loot context origin is the centre of the placed block, so y max -50.0 means the crop block itself must sit at Y-51 or lower; the description says 'below Y=-50', which is what that is. Wording changed from the idea's 'Grow' to 'Plant' because planting is what actually fires; the depth is what forces hauled dirt, water and light down there.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Chunk Miner** — Function-granted, and the cave_air hole is now closed at both ends. A carver cave or ravine leaves minecraft:cave_air, a block state distinct from minecraft:air that a player mining the chunk never touches and cannot break, so the old proof - 'the corner is air and every block equals it' - failed forever on any naturally carved chunk. Three changes. (1) The cheap slow-bucket gate moved into detect/chunk_gate, which counts five samples up the player's column (y -59, 5, 100, 200, 319) accepting air OR cave_air; y -59 exists only in the Overworld, so that sample is still the dimension check. (2) detect/chunk_scan normalises just the two diagonal corner columns with `fill ... minecraft:air replace minecraft:cave_air` (379 blocks each - a no-op for the world, since the two states behave identically) and only proceeds if both columns are then entirely air. That short-circuit is what keeps a 1x1 shaft from paying for the expensive pass. (3) detect/chunk_scan_full normalises the whole chunk in three y-slices of 32512, 32512 and 32000 blocks - each under the 32768 fill limit - and then runs the original eight-condition induction: the corner block is air, the corner column equals itself shifted one block up, three y-slices proving block(x,y,z) == block(x+1,y,z) and three more along z, each region 30480 blocks. Together every block in the chunk equals the all-air corner column. y -59..319 skips the unbreakable bedrock shell. Aquifer water still has to be drained by hand, which is part of the job.
- **Copper Loadout** — A single inventory_changed criterion whose items array holds nine separate predicates; the trigger only matches when every predicate finds a distinct stack in the inventory at the same moment, so all five copper tools and all four copper armour pieces must be carried simultaneously. Distinct from BACAP's mining/copper_golem_overlord, which is about wearing copper armour while surrounded by ten golems.
- **Deep Pockets** — Unchanged this round. inventory_changed with an item predicate on any #minecraft:shulker_boxes stack whose minecraft:container component holds a full 64-stack of Diamonds in all 27 slots - the exact structure BACAP uses for redstone/sculker_box. 1728 diamonds is three times what the parent mining/master_diamond_miner asks for and sits one frame above it.
- **Employee Badge** — Pure criteria. Structurally identical to BACAP's mining/wax_treatment (same trigger, same copper_golem entity condition) with Honeycomb swapped for a Name Tag, and identical to adventure/i_just_want_to_make_toast minus its custom_name component clause, so any name counts. Mob.checkAndHandleImportantInteractions consumes the Name Tag and returns a consuming result, which is what makes player_interacted_with_entity fire. The golem must still be alive — a fully oxidized one is a statue block and cannot be named — which is why the parent sits in the living-golem branch.
- **Freeze Frame** — Four item_used_on_block criteria, all four required (no requirements grouping, so the default AND applies). The location list carries a location_check on the unwaxed block plus a match_tool on Honeycomb, copying HD potion/alchemists_shelf and vanilla's own husbandry/wax_on - and the reason the unwaxed ID is the right one to test is that HoneycombItem fires the trigger before it swaps the block, so the state read is the pre-wax state. The player has to physically catch a block at each stage as it ages, which is the time investment. Substitution note: the original idea also demanded the four waxed blocks stand in a contiguous row; that part was dropped because it needs a region scan and the detector budget was already spent (see report).
- **Golem Courier** — FIVE Copper Golems carrying items in the same 10-tick sample, within 16 blocks — a working golem logistics network rather than the single golem doing its normal job that the first draft asked for. The medium bucket counts golems with `execute ... store result score @s bacaphd_gol if entity @e[...,limit=5]` (never `if entity @a[limit=N]`, which only tests for one match) and runs the detector only when that count is exactly 5. The detector tags each carrying golem, counts the tagged ones with another store-result, grants at five, then removes every tag it added and resets the shared counter, so nothing leaks. The carried item is looked for in BOTH weapon.mainhand and container.0 — a deliberate hedge, since no installed pack attests which slot a Copper Golem uses, and an entity that lacks a slot simply fails that test rather than erroring. Distinct from mining/copper_golem_overlord, which requires copper armour and ten idle golems and says nothing about them working.
- **Infestation** — No detection function: two bucket_medium lines. The first stores the count of silverfish within eight blocks into bacaphd_sfish with `store result score ... if entity @e[type=minecraft:silverfish,distance=..8,limit=6]` - a real count, never `if entity @e[limit=6]`, which only tests for at least one match. The second grants at six or more. The scan is bounded by both distance= and limit=, and the score is overwritten by the store on every sample before it is read, so no seeding is needed. Sampled every half second, which is ample for a chain-summoned silverfish swarm that persists for many seconds.
- **Patient Investor** — placed_block on minecraft:copper_block starts the clock; bucket_slow reads the world day once into #hd12_day and detect/patient_investor stores that day the first time it runs for a player (guarded by the tag bacaphd.pi so it can never be restarted), then ages it every cycle. The grant needs thirty in-game days on the clock AND the block directly under the player's feet to be minecraft:oxidized_copper - the unwaxed, fully weathered stage, so a waxed block is a different ID and cannot pass. Stated plainly: the exact position of the block you placed is not tracked (nothing short of a marker entity or a macro-driven coordinate could), so the block you stand on to claim it is not provably the one you placed; the thirty-day wait, which is longer than a full natural oxidation run, is what carries the 'patience' half of the idea. A player standing on generated oxidized copper in a Trial Chamber thirty days after placing a copper block would also pass.
- **Reverse Logistics** — Function-granted, sharing detect/mob_carry_watch with Landscape Gardener. Copper Golems within 24 blocks that are holding nothing get tagged bacaphd.rl_empty; on a later sample any tagged golem that is now holding something has taken an item back out of a Copper Chest, since a chest is the only place a golem's hand ever fills. Both weapon.mainhand and container.0 are tested for the held item, for the same reason HD's existing detect/golem_courier tests both: no installed pack attests which slot a Copper Golem uses, and an entity without a slot simply fails that test. The chest itself is not block-checked — the empty-to-full transition is already unique to a withdrawal.
- **Shock Treatment** — minecraft:lightning_strike fires for every player near a bolt; the lightning predicate is the whole check. stepping_on reads the block under the bolt's feet, and a bolt is spawned standing on the surface it hits, so stepping_on = the struck block. Requiring that block to be Oxidized Copper is exactly the condition under which vanilla strips its oxidation, so the patina loss is guaranteed by the same event that grants it. distance.absolute.max 16 keeps it to a strike the player is actually present for, copying blazeandcave:mining/called_shot, which uses the identical lightning-predicate shape. The idea sheet said 'beside' a copper block; I tightened the description to 'an Oxidized Copper Block' because the criterion requires a direct hit, which is what does the stripping.
- **Time Machine** — Pure criteria, three item_used_on_block criteria AND-ed by the default requirements. Correction to the idea, which asked for four: oxidized to plain copper is three scrapes, not four. The location_check reads the block AFTER the use resolves — this is BACAP's building/stripper shape, which matches stripped_oak_log, the result block — so the three criteria are the three RESULTS: weathered_copper, exposed_copper, copper_block. The seven axes are enumerated rather than using #minecraft:axes, matching how the rest of this pack avoids unattested tags. Stated plainly: scraping wax off waxed_weathered_copper or waxed_exposed_copper produces the same result blocks, so a player with waxed copper could satisfy two of the three criteria by de-waxing instead of de-oxidizing; oxidation stage is a separate block ID, not a block state, so no predicate can tell the two apart.
- **Torchbearer** — The scoreboard objective bacaphd_ctorch tracks minecraft.used:minecraft.copper_torch, which vanilla increments each time the BlockItem is placed. The slow bucket grants directly to any player whose score reaches 64. The selector carries gamemode=!spectator AND gamemode=!creative, because this measures block placement and creative torches are free. This is a counting task, not a restatement of BACAP's building/chartreuse (one-off 'light up the area').
- **Urban Decay** — Detector 4 of 5, hybrid with criteria. The `city` criterion is a plain minecraft:location structure test on minecraft:ancient_city with BACAP's inverted-spectator guard; the impossible criterion sits in its OWN requirements group (an AND, never one group containing both) so criteria alone can never complete it. The bucket line selects on advancements={...={city=true,impossible=false}}, so the detector runs only while the player stands in a city and stops the instant the whole advancement is granted. It revokes `city` to re-arm, then credits the site only if it is 512+ blocks (Chebyshev) from BOTH previously credited cities, so shuttling between two cannot pay for a third. The branch is taken on bacaphd_ud_p, a snapshot of the count, so one visit can never advance two states.
- **Vein Miner** — Unchanged this round. Function-granted. bucket_slow fires when either minecraft.mined:minecraft.diamond_ore or minecraft.mined:minecraft.deepslate_diamond_ore changes; detect/vein_miner consumes both deltas on every visit, then adds them to the running count. The window is stamped from the FIRST ore of the run in bacaphd_vein_start, so nine ores fifty-nine seconds apart no longer accumulate; a run whose first ore is more than 1200 game ticks old is discarded and the next ore starts a fresh one. Ore broken in creative is thrown away and ends the run. The count is reset on the grant path.
- **Vertical Limit** — Two medium-bucket lines. Standing in the overworld bedrock layer (`positioned ~ -64 ~ if entity @s[dx=0,dy=6,dz=0]`) arms a 120-sample timer; detect/vl_top then decrements that timer once per 10 ticks and reads the exact altitude, granting at y>=319, so the run expires after exactly 60 seconds. The top test is no longer a selector volume. A dx/dy/dz selector box matches on bounding-box INTERSECTION, not on the feet position, so `positioned ~ 319 ~ if entity @s[dy=2000]` passed as soon as the player's head crossed 319, i.e. with the feet at about y 317.2 -- almost two blocks early. detect/vl_top now stores Pos[1] with scale 1 and compares the score, which is exact. The reviewer's stated cause (Vec3Argument centre-correcting the integer) is not the reason: WorldCoordinates.parseDouble passes centerCorrect=false for the Y component specifically, which is why /tp @s 0 64 0 lands you at 0.5, 64.0, 0.5 -- the Y anchor was already 319.0 and -64.0 exactly. The bedrock arming line keeps the volume idiom: the same intersection slack there only widens the arming zone to feet between about -65.8 and -57, which is the bedrock layer plus a block, and being generous at the START of a 380-block climb costs nothing. No dimension check is needed beyond the overworld guard on the arming line since y>=319 is unreachable in the Nether or the End. Creative is excluded alongside spectator, and the timer is zeroed on the grant path.
- **Weathering Heights** — DETECTOR 4 of 5. Adjacency of four specific blocks has no criterion. detect/weathering_heights scans 11 x 5 x 11 around the player and each position only ever asks whether it is the START of a four-block run, so all but a handful of positions fail on the first `if block` and cost one test. Four chains per position cover both axes and both reading directions, so the gradient does not have to face any particular way. It grants on bacaphd_whh >= 1 and runs at bucket_slow behind @r, so at most one player pays for it per five-second cycle. Waxed variants deliberately do not count: waxing is what stops the oxidation this advancement is about, and Sly Copper already rewards the waxed set.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Bad Medicine** — One effects_changed criterion. The effects map is checked against the player's whole current effect set, so it only passes when all three are running at once, and the source list (the same shape BACAP uses in 'Thanks a lotl') pins the effect that completed the set to a witch. A witch throws Slowness beyond 8 blocks, Poison above 8 health and Weakness at close range, so all three together is a real fight, not a lucky tick.
- **Caught in My Own Web** — enter_block on minecraft:cobweb - the same trigger BACAP redstone/tripping_over uses on tripwire, another walk-through block - with an entity_properties condition requiring minecraft:weaving on the player, matching the `effects` map BACAP potion/marine_marauder uses. Reworded from the original 'a Cobweb that your own Weaving effect spun': Weaving only spawns cobwebs when the affected entity dies, so a player could never both spin the web and then walk into it while still holding the effect. What is checked is the achievable half - being the one wearing Weaving while inside a web - which needs an Ominous-source Weaving on yourself rather than on a mob, and nothing in the four packs covers that.
- **Cell Block** — Function-granted. bucket_slow picks at most one candidate player per five-second cycle with @r, tags the twelve two-block-tall hostiles within 12 blocks, then tests the nearest one's cell: four walls at foot level, four at head level, a floor and a ceiling. Each face is one execute with eight chained `unless block` clauses - one per Copper Bar oxidation stage, waxed and unwaxed - so a face that is any kind of Copper Bar leaves the fail flag alone. The flag starts at 1 and is only cleared once a candidate mob is confirmed to exist, so an empty scan can never grant.
- **Death by Committee** — Unchanged this round. Ten entity_hurt_player criteria (blocked:false, so shield hits do not count) plus an eleventh, `armed`, which fires on damage from ANY entity - source_entity present but empty. All eleven are required and `armed` is implied by every one of the ten, so it costs the player nothing. The first time `armed` shows up, detect/committee_arm starts a fixed thirty second countdown (60 medium ticks) and detect/committee_tick revokes the whole advancement's progress when it runs out; nothing refreshes it. A separate bucket line fires detect/committee_reset the moment the health objective reads 0, so 'and survive' is enforced. Every line that can revoke is gated on advancements={...=false} and committee_arm parks the timer at -1 once the advancement is held.
- **Door to Door** — Detection function bacaphd:detect/door_to_door from bucket_medium. There is no `execute if difficulty`, so the Hard gate is the Zombie's own CanBreakDoors NBT byte, which vanilla only sets on Hard — the exact flag that permits the behaviour. Each door-breaking Zombie's four horizontal neighbours are checked against #minecraft:wooden_doors and a minecraft:marker is dropped in that block (one per block, guarded by `unless entity @e[...,distance=..0.9]`). When a marked block is no longer a door and a CanBreakDoors Zombie is still within 3 blocks, the watching player is granted and the marker is killed. An ungated bucket_slow line kills any marker with no breaker left within 4 blocks, so markers cannot accumulate even after the advancement is held. False positive worth naming: breaking that same door yourself while the Zombie is still on it would also fire.
- **Identity Crisis** — The exact inverse of blazeandcave:monsters/camouflage, built from the same two pieces: minecraft:entity_killed_player (BACAP uses it in monsters/aww_man, where `entity` is the killer) plus a `player` entity predicate carrying equipment.head, the object form BACAP uses in monsters/lancelot. Five head/killer pairs, requirements a single OR group so any one pair completes it. The trigger fires from ServerPlayer.die() before the inventory is dropped, so the worn head is still there when the predicate runs. The piglin pair is included for completeness but is close to unusable in practice - wearing a piglin head pacifies piglins - so the real routes are the creeper head (charged-creeper kill first), zombie head, skeleton skull and wither skeleton skull.
- **Landscape Gardener** — Function-granted, sharing detect/mob_carry_watch with Reverse Logistics. Endermen within 24 blocks that have a carriedBlockState get tagged bacaphd.lg_carry; on a later sample a tagged Enderman with no carriedBlockState has just put its block down, and the player watching gets the grant. carriedBlockState is only written while the Enderman is actually carrying, which is what BACAP's monsters/basketblock_championship keys its own entity NBT predicate off. The pick-up half is implied rather than separately proven: the tag is only ever applied to an Enderman inside the player's 24-block radius, so the player was there while it was carrying.
- **Leaving a Mess** — Pure criteria. minecraft:entity_killed_player with a player predicate requiring the Oozing effect to be active at the moment of death; the two slimes are what the effect does on death, so no separate check is wanted or possible. entity_killed_player needs a killing MOB, so drowning or falling with Oozing does not count -- which matches 'leave a pair of slimes behind you' in a fight. The empty-effect-object form {"minecraft:oozing":{}} is the one already used in bacaphd:challenges/naked_and_afraid.
- **Overtime** — Pure criteria: a pickaxe losing durability while the player holds Mining Fatigue at amplifier 2, which only an Elder Guardian hands out. The effects/amplifier form is BACAP's Maximum Resistance, the item_durability_changed form is Some Breakthrough. There is no vanilla block-broken trigger, so tool damage is the stand-in, and it does not distinguish mining from hitting something with the pickaxe - the gate that actually costs the player anything is holding Mining Fatigue III at all.
- **Pacifist Streak** — A bacaphd_kills objective on the vanilla `totalKillCount` criterion is compared against its own private mirror every 100 ticks; unchanged adds 100 ticks to the streak, any change wipes it, and 120000 ticks (five in-game days of online time) grants it. detect/init_b2 seeds the mirror from the live statistic on the player's first tick so a lifetime kill count is never read as a fresh kill. The whole check is four scoreboard commands per player per five seconds and touches no NBT; the streak is zeroed on the grant path. Unchanged this round.
- **Pyrrhic Victory** — Unchanged this round. Function-granted. bucket_fast watches the minecraft.killed:minecraft.wither statistic against bacaphd_wither_seen_pv - a mirror owned by THIS advancement alone, so it can neither consume nor be consumed by any other Wither-watching check in the pack. The tick a Wither kill is credited, detect/pyrrhic_victory grants if the health objective reads 0, i.e. the player is already dead at that instant. The mirror is rebased on every visit and the housekeeping line is not gated on the advancement, so a lifetime total can never bank up.
- **Rain Check** — Substitute for Possum Impression, which needed an Axolotl's play-dead state and that state is not written to entity NBT, so no detector could see it. This one is pure criteria: minecraft:player_hurt_entity where the victim is an Enderman and the direct damage entity is a thrown minecraft:splash_potion - the entity type, confirmed as an entity id by Enhanced Discoveries' adventure/water_dependent, which matches looking_at against it. Water is the only splash potion that hurts an Enderman without also being a kill in one throw, and any damage at all makes an Enderman teleport, so the hurt event and the escape are the same instant. Damage-type tags are left off on purpose; the projectile identity is the specific part.
- **Skin Crawler** — entity_hurt_player with damage.source_entity typed as a Silverfish - the same damage/source_entity structure BACAP's 'The Nether's Shield' uses - plus an effects predicate on the player (the form ED's wear_dragon_vs_warden predicate uses) requiring minecraft:infested to still be active. That is what ties the bite to the effect's own spawn rather than to a silverfish out of a stone block: Infested outlasts the damage tick that spawned the swarm, so the retaliation lands inside it.
- **Sniffed Out** — One bucket_medium line, no detection function. `execute as <player> at @s as @e[type=minecraft:warden,distance=..24] on target unless entity @s[type=minecraft:player] run advancement grant @p[distance=..1] ...`. `on target` reads the Warden's live attack target and yields no executor at all when it has none, so the guard is exact rather than inferred. `on` moves the executor but never the position, so the grant selector still resolves at the player's own coordinates - @p[distance=..1] is that player and nobody else.
- **Staring Contest** — One polled minecraft:location criterion carrying two things at once: an equipment.head item test (the shape ED uses in bacaped:nether/bastion_sweet_bastion) and the type_specific/player looking_at predicate with an absolute distance cap, copied field-for-field from BACAP's monsters/look_out. The location trigger polls roughly once a second, so the stare has to be held for about a second rather than flicked past - which is the whole point, since the pumpkin is what stops the Enderman aggroing while you do it. The 16-block cap keeps it a stare-down rather than a long-range spot.
- **Terraforming Service** — bucket_medium gates on one Enderman being in range at all, then detect/terraforming_service counts properly with `execute store result score ... if entity @e[...limit=3]` rather than `if entity @e[limit=3]`, which would only test for at least one. Carrying is detected by the presence of the carried block NBT key, matched with an empty compound so any block value passes. Both spellings are counted and summed - carried_block_state (current) and carriedBlockState (pre-1.21.5) - because an Enderman can only ever hold one of the two, so nothing is double counted and the check survives whichever name this server writes. Not confident about which spelling this exact version uses, which is precisely why both are read.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Backdraft** — Two criteria ANDed as two separate requirement groups: placing a bed in the_nether (placed_block + location_check with block and dimension, the shape BACAP's 'Hot Chocolate' uses) and taking explosion damage in the_nether (entity_hurt_player with damage.type.tags is_explosion, the shape ED uses for its void death). Honest limitations: the damage type predicate has no field for an exact damage type id, so minecraft:bad_respawn_point cannot be named directly — a ghast fireball would also satisfy the second half, which is why the bed placement is required as well; and no criterion can tell death from damage, so the description says 'blow yourself up' rather than 'get killed'. Clicking the bed itself is untestable because the block is removed before the trigger reads the position.
- **Bed Bath and Beyond** — Pure criteria: unblocked explosion damage taken inside minecraft:bastion_remnant. damage.type.tags is Dimension Penetration's shape and location.structures is From Whence It Came!'s. Two honest caveats. The damage-type predicate only exposes tags, not a type id, so any explosion inside a bastion qualifies - in practice a bed or a respawn anchor, since creepers do not spawn in the Nether, though a stray Ghast fireball would also do it. And entity_hurt_player fires on the damage tick whether or not the blast kills you, so 'live through it' is flavour rather than something the criterion enforces.
- **Colour Coordinated** — Sixteen minecraft:tick criteria, one per colour, in a single OR requirements group that covers all sixteen names exactly - the player only has to match one colour. Each criterion asks, on the tick, that the player's four leather armour slots all carry the exact `minecraft:dyed_color` integer for that dye AND that their vehicle is a Happy Ghast whose body slot holds the same-coloured harness. The sixteen integers are not computed, they are lifted straight out of BACAP animal/shoe_shed, which asserts them as the result of dyeing a leather item with one dye of that colour. The tick trigger with a `player` list-of-conditions is Enhanced Discoveries adventure/water_dependent; equipment slot keys head/chest/legs/feet/body and `vehicle` are all attested in BACAP advancements (weaponry/hit_and_run uses `vehicle`, predicate/equipment/*.json uses `body`). Because `components` is an exact match, armour dyed twice or with mixed dyes will not qualify - that is intentional, the point is a clean single-dye match.
- **Fortress of Solitude** — The structure test is done by vanilla, not by a function: `inside` is an ordinary minecraft:location criterion whose predicate names minecraft:fortress, copying BACAP nether/midport_village's shape including its spectator exclusion. The game re-evaluates the location trigger for every player every 20 ticks, so detect/fortress_of_solitude revokes that one criterion on every 100-tick sample and finding it back on the next sample proves the player never left. 240 unbroken samples is 24000 ticks — one Minecraft day — and any sample that finds it absent resets the counter to zero. The `impossible` guard is in its own requirements group so the location criterion can never complete the advancement on its own.
- **Fungus Among Us** — No detection function: two bucket_fast lines following the pack's established Ominous Marathon mirror pattern. Placing a block increments minecraft.used: for its item, so bacaphd_wfung (minecraft.used:minecraft.warped_fungus) moving on a given tick means the fungus went down on that tick; the credit is taken there and then, with a hoglin required within eight blocks - a Hoglin's warped-fungus flee radius - reading the player's position at the instant of the placement. The resync line runs ungated for every player so no advancement state can desynchronise the mirror, and it runs after the credit so exactly one credit is taken per placement. The mirror is seeded in seed_lines before the first read. Caveat: minecraft.used:minecraft.warped_fungus also moves when warped fungus is used on a Strider, so feeding a Strider with a Hoglin standing eight blocks away would also pass.
- **Ghast Couture** — FIXED for ownership. The colour tags still live on the ghast so they survive relogs, but two things changed. (1) The tag pass no longer runs for anyone standing near a ghast; it only runs for a player who is RIDING it, reached with `on vehicle` — the same hop BACAP uses in biomes/submarine_fleet. A Happy Ghast can only be ridden once a Harness has been fitted (BACAP's nether/hot_air_balloon: 'Craft a Harness, place it on a Happy Ghast, and ride it'), so 'ridden while it wore this colour' is a faithful stand-in for 'fitted with this colour' and costs the player nothing they were not already doing. (2) The grant is gated on bacaphd.h_new, a marker the tag pass sets only when the colour worn this sample is one the ghast has never worn before. So the grant belongs to the rider who registered the sixteenth colour; a second player who mounts the finished ghast registers no new colour and is not granted. The player's identity is carried across the `on vehicle` hop by the short-lived bacaphd.gc_self tag, because after `on vehicle` the executor is the ghast — going back with `on passengers` would have granted every passenger, up to four people. A body slot holds exactly one item, so at most one colour can match per pass, which is what makes the newness marker exact. `if items entity @s armor.body *` is used rather than a guessed `#minecraft:harnesses` tag; `body` is the equipment slot BACAP reads for Nautilus and Horse armour in blazeandcave:predicate/equipment/.
- **Ghast Nursery** — DETECTOR. A Ghastling is a baby minecraft:happy_ghast - BACAP nether/snow_cone identifies it exactly that way, with flags.is_baby. Every 0.5 s a player who lacks the advancement and has a Snowball in the main hand (`if items entity @s weapon.mainhand`, already used in this pack's bucket_slow) counts happy_ghasts within 8 blocks; each one's Age NBT is read into a score and only negative ages - babies - are counted, so adults are excluded. Three or more grants. The Snowball requirement is in the description because it is genuinely part of the test, and it is also what bounds the cost: without it the entity count would run for every player every half second.
- **Growing Pains** — Pure criteria - this is the one place I abandoned the suggested function. Three AND-groups: place a waterlogged Dried Ghast (the exact placed_block + block_state_property shape of vanilla husbandry/place_dried_ghast_in_water), feed a snowball to a Happy Ghast whose entity predicate has flags.is_baby true (a Ghastling), and fit any of the sixteen Harnesses on a Happy Ghast with flags.is_baby false - which only an adult accepts. The 16 harness criteria are ONE OR-group, so the third group is a single genuine step. Honest limitation: the criteria cannot bind all three legs to the same individual ghast, which the function version would have. In practice a solo player rearing one ghast does exactly this sequence, and I judged that worth trading against a fourth detector.
- **Hunting Party** — SUBSTITUTE for 'Equal Opportunity Offender' (see report). Detector detect/hunting_party, per tick from bucket_fast because a Hoglin's DeathTime reads 1 for exactly one tick and cannot be sampled slower. It counts Piglins with `store result ... if entity @e[type=piglin,distance=..16,limit=3]`, then walks `as @e[type=hoglin,distance=..16,limit=1,nbt={DeathTime:1s}] on attacker if entity @s[type=piglin]`. `on attacker` resolves to the entity that last damaged the Hoglin, so it both proves the Piglins made the kill and rules out the player claiming their own — no kill-statistic mirror and therefore no seeding hazard at all. The player is tagged bacaphd.hp_here for the two lines it takes to get the grant back out of the Hoglin's execution context, and untagged again immediately. DeathTime is a short, hence `1s`; an int literal would silently never match. The 'dance over the kill' half of the original idea is dropped — Piglin celebration is not exposed in NBT — and the description was reworded accordingly.
- **Magma Marathon** — FIXED for the same reason the reviewer raised against Dragon Rider: the description promises 'without taking a single point of damage' and the counter was merely PAUSING on damage, not clearing. bacaphd_lava is now zeroed the moment minecraft.custom:minecraft.damage_taken moves, so the five hundred blocks really do have to be unbroken. Every 10 ticks the medium bucket samples X/Z in decimetres; if the feet block is lava and the damage mirror has not moved since the previous sample, the horizontal step is added to bacaphd_lava. The damage mirror bacaphd_mm_dmg is private to this advancement and is seeded from the player's current lifetime total by detect/init_b4 on first sight, and the bucket line refuses to run until that seeding has happened. Steps over 40 blocks per sample are discarded so teleports cannot pad the total, and bacaphd_lava is zeroed on the grant path.
- **Portal Sniper** — Unchanged this round. Entering the Nether freezes the Overworld anchor AND records the arrival position; every subsequent Nether sample updates bacaphd_ps_far with the furthest the player has been from that arrival point (a running max, not a path length, so milling around next to the portal banks nothing). The return check only grants when bacaphd_ps_far is at least 200 blocks and the Overworld arrival is within 10 blocks of the departure point. This is deliberately not 'link two different portals precisely' - vanilla portal search makes a far-flung second Nether portal land 8x further out in the Overworld, so that version is unachievable. bacaphd_ps_far is reset on the grant path and on every fresh entry.
- **Roof Runner** — FIXED - elytra can no longer bank it at any altitude. The reviewer is right that 'unless block ~ ~-1 ~ minecraft:air' at feet y 128 tests the bedrock roof itself and is therefore always true, so gliding one to five blocks above the roof passed. Two changes replace it: the altitude band is now y 128..129 only (feet on the roof surface, or at the apex of a jump from it - a glider has to stay inside a two-block slot right against the bedrock), and the per-sample step is capped at 6 blocks per half second, i.e. 12 blocks a second. Sprinting is 5.6 and sprint-jumping about 7.1, so foot travel counts in full; unassisted gliding is 15 to 25 and rocket flight 30 plus, so neither contributes anything. The redundant block test under the feet is gone - in the Nether there is nowhere at y 128 except above the bedrock ceiling, which tops out at y 127. The bucket line still carries gamemode=!spectator,gamemode=!creative. bacaphd_roof is zeroed on the grant path.
- **Sky Ferry** — bucket_medium walks `on vehicle` to confirm the vehicle is a minecraft:happy_ghast and `on passengers` back to a player sitting on it - the same shape HD already uses for Copper Stable - then detect/sky_ferry counts non-player entities within 12 blocks that carry a leash record, reading both `leash` and `Leash` and summing them (a mob holds only one, so nothing is double counted). Four of them plus open air 4 and 8 blocks below the rider is a ghast in flight with a full payload. Stated plainly: the idea's 'four DIFFERENT mobs' clause is NOT enforced - proving four distinct species would mean enumerating types - so the description says four leashed mobs, which is what the check actually requires. A mob leashed to a fence directly under the ghast could in principle be counted, but it would have to be within 12 blocks of a rider who has open air 8 blocks below.
- **Soul Speed Demon** — FIXED - the boots are now re-proven continuously instead of once. The tick bucket still selects on advancements={bacaphd:nether/soul_speed_demon={soul_speed=true,distance=false}}, but the first line of detect/soul_speed_demon revokes that criterion again, so the game has to re-award it before the next sample can accumulate anything. The vanilla minecraft:location trigger is polled once a second per player, so in practice one sample in two banks distance and the boots are verified about once a second for the whole five kilometres; take them off and accumulation stops within a second. This is Enhanced Discoveries' own revoke-to-re-prove idiom - bacaped:1sec_timer revokes camel_adventure, big_pig_adventure and intergalactic_journey exactly this way and relies on their location criteria being re-earned, and flap_dont_fall_reset uses the criterion-level 'advancement revoke @s only <adv> <criterion>' form. The revoke is deliberately the FIRST line so the completion path's grant (which grants both criteria at once) can never be undone by it in the same execution. The per-sample cap is tightened from 40 blocks to 25, matching the slower effective sample rate. The criterion predicate itself is BACAP's nether/soul_runnings shape. Both criteria are required (no requirements block). The accumulator is zeroed on the grant path.
- **Soul Survivor** — A single minecraft:location criterion whose location predicate carries both structures and biomes, exactly the shape BACAP's 'Gold Mine!' uses for structure-in-an-unusual-biome, with the same inverted-spectator guard. Both fields sit on the same predicate so they are ANDed: the player must be inside the fortress bounding box while the biome under their feet is Soul Sand Valley.
- **Stable Genius** — Three bucket_medium lines, no detect function. Line one parks bacaphd_sg_y at -1 for anyone who still lacks it; line two overwrites it with the player's own Y (`store result ... run data get entity @s Pos[1]`) but only while the player is in the_nether with a hoglin inside twelve blocks; line three grants at bacaphd_sg_y 128 or above. Because the score is reset every cycle, a stale reading can never grant. Two honest reductions from the idea's mechanism: the 'was in a bastion' history and the 'leashed by you' test are both dropped. The bastion flag needs per-hoglin persistent state, and a leash test would have to match an NBT key name I cannot verify against this corpus - a wrong key means the check silently never fires, which is worse than not checking. Neither costs much: hoglins only generate in crimson forests and bastions and have no way onto the roof by themselves, so a hoglin standing beside you above Y128 is a hoglin you walked up there.
- **Static Discharge** — One minecraft:entity_hurt_player criterion: damage.source_entity is the lightning bolt entity (the same damage.source_entity field blazeandcave:monsters/blast_shield uses for a creeper), and the player predicate requires the happy ghast as vehicle. No weather check is needed - lightning only falls in a thunderstorm, and riding a ghast above the terrain is what makes you the tallest thing around. Five points of damage, so you live through it.
- **The Floor Is Lava** — Function-granted. Sampled every 10 ticks in the Nether only, as players who are neither spectator nor creative. A sample earns credit only when the player is on the ground, is not standing in lava, has a solid block directly underfoot, and has lava two to four blocks below that - which is what walking a bridge across a lava sea looks like. `execute on vehicle` zeroes the sample, so a Strider ride earns nothing, and elytra or levitation fail the OnGround test. Distance uses the pack's existing octagonal max + 3/8*min approximation in decimetres; 2000 grants. Any failing sample resets the run to zero and re-seeds the previous position so the restart cannot bank a teleport. It cannot verify the blocks were ones the player placed - it verifies the player crossed on foot, dry - so I dropped 'using only blocks you place' from the description.
- **Under the Bridge** — A polled minecraft:location criterion combining nether/jenga's nested `vehicle.entity_type` with ED's location.structure gate on minecraft:bastion_remnant. Riding a strider means the lava sea, and the bastion's bounding box covers the lava under a bridge bastion, so the intended route is exactly what the idea described. I did NOT add a lava fluid test, and the description no longer claims one: a ridden player sits well above the lava surface, and even the strider's own block position sits at the boundary of the lava column rather than inside it, so a fluid predicate could easily have made this uncompletable. Rather than ship a check I cannot be confident in, the criterion tests what it can prove - a strider ridden inside a Bastion Remnant.

</details>

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Alchemist's Shelf** — Twenty-three item_used_on_block criteria, one per base Potion type, each requiring the used item to be a minecraft:potion whose potion_contents matches that type (the 'predicates':{'potion_contents': ...} item-predicate form BACAP uses in Furious Ammunition) and the clicked block to be one of the twelve wood Shelves; the location/match_tool condition-list shape is copied from blazeandcave:adventure/im_not_lost_anymore. Long and strong variants are deliberately excluded so the set stays at one bottle per effect. The description says 'Place every type of Potion onto Shelves' - plural, accumulated over time - because a single Shelf holds only three items and because the criteria latch on each placement rather than on the potions still being there.
- **Bad Trip** — A single effects_changed criterion, written with the trigger's top-level 'effects' map as in blazeandcave:adventure/war_is_coming, that fires when the player simultaneously holds Nausea (pufferfish or a Nether portal), Blindness (a witch or suspicious stew) and Darkness (a Sculk Shrieker or a Warden).
- **Bottle Service** — One item_used_on_block criterion. BACAP's 'Bubble, Bubble, Toil and Trouble' proves cauldron interactions fire this trigger AND that the block state it reports is the state AFTER the interaction (it matches water_cauldron after a bucket fill on an empty cauldron), so the condition here is level=3 with a water bottle as the tool — the third bottle. Level 2 is unreachable with a bucket, so requiring the bottle that takes it to 3 does prove an all-bottle fill; the one cheese is filling by bucket, drawing one bottle off and putting it back. The state map uses the string form and the potion_contents list form, both attested in the reference packs.
- **Breaking Bad** — The brewed_potion criterion is a doorbell, not the completion: requirements are AND-of-groups so holding `brewed` alone can never finish the advancement. bucket_fast sees `brewed=true, impossible=false`, revokes `brewed` first so the very next bottle can ring it again, adds one to bacaphd_brews, and grants at 1000. A fresh counter seeded to 0 at first login, never a lifetime statistic, so it measures brewing done after install. Per-tick sampling is deliberate: brewed_potion fires once per finished bottle pulled off the stand, and a 10-tick sample would drop two of every three-bottle batch.
- **Call It Off** — minecraft:consume_item on minecraft:milk_bucket with an entity_properties condition requiring the Raid Omen effect - the same item+effect pairing BACAP uses in monsters/miracle_drink. The ordering that makes it work is inside MilkBucketItem.finishUsingItem: it fires the consume criterion first and only then calls removeAllEffects, so the predicate still sees Raid Omen at the moment it is evaluated. Fires once, on the swallow that defuses the raid.
- **Chemical Dependency** — DETECTOR 5 of 5, and the cheapest one in the pack. Sustaining a floor over time needs a timer, and counting effects has no predicate - but `data get entity @s active_effects` on an NBT LIST returns its length, so the entire count is one command with no per-effect enumeration to keep in step with the game. active_effects is the path this project already reads in bacaptmh's group_buff and community_beacon detectors. Called from bucket_medium (every 10 ticks), the timer adds 10 while the count is 8 or more and is SET back to 0 the moment it is not, so 24000 means one in-game day held unbroken. Two ungated lines in bucket_medium also zero the timer for anyone in creative or spectator, so progress cannot be banked with /effect. If a player has no effects at all the path is absent, the command fails and `execute store result` writes 0 - which is the correct answer anyway.
- **Drinking Problem** — Pure criteria. consume_item with an item/player pair is BACAP's Miracle Drink; the fluid predicate is I'm in Lava With You with water swapped in. The head test is a minecraft:location_check at offsetY 1 - the loot origin in an advancement context is the player's position, so offset 1 is the block the head occupies - using the offsetX/Y/Z form Enhanced Discoveries uses in its australia_pig predicate. Both the feet block and the head block must be water, which is what 'completely underwater' means.
- **Have Stand, Will Travel** — Three brewed_potion criteria, all required (default AND). BACAP potion/medic attests the trigger; the dimension rides in the standard entity_properties + location predicate that BACAP nether/midport_village uses. brewed_potion fires when the player takes the potion out of the stand's output slot, so the player is standing at the stand and the location predicate reads the dimension the stand is in.
- **Hexed** — Pure criteria, no tick cost. Three independent minecraft:effects_changed criteria, all required. The `source` field carries the entity that caused the change, and is written exactly as BACAP writes it in animal/thanks_a_lotl; requiring entity_type minecraft:witch means a brewed potion you drank yourself cannot satisfy any of them. The three do not have to land at the same time, which matches the wording of the idea - a Witch picks its potion by range and by your health, so collecting all three is a matter of fighting one at several distances.
- **Mise en Place** — One inventory_changed with three item predicates in the `items` list; vanilla requires every entry in that list to be satisfied, and `count` is matched against a single stack's size (the form ED's 'So Bright' uses for 64-count diamonds), so this is three full stacks held simultaneously. All three items stack to 64, so 'a stack' is unambiguous.
- **Net Zero** — Pure criteria. killing_blow.tags is_player_attack is the melee test (same shape as blazeandcave:monsters/lancelot); the effects map requires Strength at amplifier 1 or better (Strength II) and Weakness simultaneously; the empty hand is the inverted equipment.mainhand.count.min=1 idiom already shipped in bacaphd:challenges/naked_and_afraid -- an empty stack has count 0, so the inversion holds only for a truly bare hand. Amplifier is a minimum rather than exactly 1, so Strength III from a beacon stack also qualifies; the gimmick is the same either way.
- **Off the Wagon** — Pure criteria and zero objectives — no /trigger plumbing needed after all. minecraft:location polled against a player stats predicate, exactly BACAP's statistics/captain_nemo shape, with `type: minecraft:used` and `stat: minecraft:potion` (the item-used counter, which is what drinking a potion increments) as Enhanced Discoveries does in end/endergardener. Splash and lingering potions are separate item IDs and so do not count. This IS an absolute lifetime statistic and a veteran world could already be part-way there; that is deliberate and acceptable here because the advancement is explicitly a grind, the same reading BACAP gives animal/wheres_the_honey_lebowski.
- **Overdose** — One effects_changed criterion using the trigger's own top-level 'effects' map, the shape BACAP ships in blazeandcave:potion/a_much_more_doable_challenge and Enhanced Discoveries ships in challenges/explosive_elexir. The axis moved from 'how many effects' to 'where they came from'. Twelve effects must be held at once AND each must still have at least 600 ticks left (MobEffectInstancePredicate.duration, verified in the 26.1.2 class files). A beacon writes its effect with a duration of (9 + levels*2) seconds - 340 ticks at a full pyramid - Conduit Power is 260 ticks and Dolphin's Grace 100, so none of the ambient sources can ever show 600 and every one of the twelve has to come out of a bottle. That makes this a brewing-throughput challenge instead of a subset of 'How Did We Get Here?': twelve different brews stood up and drunk inside about twenty seconds. Regeneration is the only member of the list whose base brew (0:45) is too short once drinking time is counted, so the extended version is needed; Absorption comes from a Golden Apple (2:00). The old fifteen-effect list was nether/all_effects with the cheap half removed, and is gone.

</details>

| ◆ | **Under Observation** | Redstone | Set an Observer down beside a Shelf, a Copper Chest and a Copper Bulb | 45 |

<details><summary>How these are detected</summary>

- **Around the Clock** — Eight placed_block criteria in two OR groups, ANDed. The first group is the clock loop: place a Hopper whose facing state points at a neighbouring Hopper whose facing points straight back - one criterion per horizontal axis direction. The second group is the readout: place a Comparator whose back (the side opposite its facing) is against a Hopper. The offsetX/offsetZ form on location_check is the same one blazeandcave:biomes/pale_in_comparison uses. No region scan and no function; the two halves are proved by the acts of placing them, so a player could in principle place the Comparator against a different Hopper than the clock's - acceptable slack at task tier.
- **Cannonball** — REWORKED into an impossible-gated advancement, the shape Enhanced uses for end/dragon_blitz. The `cannonball` criterion is the same fall_after_explosion / TNT cause / distance.horizontal.min 100 as before, now with the same is_flying:false glide guard as Wind Rider; the second criterion is minecraft:impossible and requirements are [["cannonball"],["impossible"]], so the advancement can never complete on its own. Survival is no longer tested inside the criterion, because it cannot be: fall_after_explosion is fired from checkFallDamage BEFORE the landing damage is applied, and the health-criterion objective is only rewritten later in the same player tick, so at criterion time bacaphd_health still holds pre-landing health either way. Instead a bucket_fast line matches `advancements={bacaphd:redstone/cannonball={cannonball=true,impossible=false}}` and runs detect/cannonball. #minecraft:tick functions run at the top of tickChildren, before the levels tick, so that line first sees the completed criterion on the tick AFTER the landing -- by which point bacaphd_health has been rewritten with the post-landing value. 1 or more grants the whole advancement (`advancement grant @s only` completes the impossible criterion too); 0 revokes it, which re-arms the criterion for the next attempt. Once granted, impossible=true and the selector stops matching, so the line self-retires.
- **Circular Reasoning** — One detect function, dispatched from bucket_slow through @r so at most one candidate player is scanned per five seconds no matter how many lack it - the same bounded-region-scan discipline the pack already uses for Sorting Facility, Clock Tower and Monochrome. It tests every 2x2 flat Observer ring whose origin falls in the 3x3 footprint at the player's feet level or one block below, in both rotational senses: 36 short-circuiting `if block` chains, each demanding four minecraft:observer blocks whose facing values chain head to tail round the square. Testing both senses is deliberate insurance: whichever way the vanilla `facing` state is read (the observed face or the output face), one of the two patterns is the real working ring, so the check is correct either way. It does mean an anticlockwise ring and a clockwise ring both count, which is right anyway - both are working clocks.
- **Clock Tower** — FIXED - the reviewer's exploit build (a lever on a bell, a detector on the ground and eight dust sprinkled five blocks away) no longer passes, because the check is now vertical. The scan volume changed from 11x7x11 to 9x11x9 - narrower horizontally, much taller - and the loop's own y index doubles as an altitude reading: bacaphd_ct_by keeps the lowest layer holding a powered Bell and bacaphd_ct_dy the highest layer holding an EMITTING Daylight Detector. The grant needs a powered Bell, an emitting Detector, eight pieces of redstone dust / repeaters / comparators in the volume, and the Detector at least five layers ABOVE the Bell. A flat one-room build cannot produce that separation, and a two-block Detector-on-Bell stack certainly cannot; what can is an actual tower with the sensor on the roof and the bell below it, which is what the advancement is named after. It still cannot follow the wire from one end to the other - no command can - and the description now states exactly the three things that are measured rather than implying a timed circuit. The selector carries gamemode=!spectator,gamemode=!creative and every counter is reset at the top of each scan; the two altitude registers are seeded to 99 and -99 so a missing Bell or Detector yields a negative separation and fails.
- **Copper Counter** — Unchanged this round. A 13x5x13 slow-bucket scan around one candidate player counts Copper Bulbs in all eight oxidation and waxed variants, split by their lit blockstate; sixteen bulbs in total with at least four lit and at least four unlit grants it. The mixed pattern stands in for 'working counter', which is not something a command can verify. The air short-circuit skips air, cave_air and void_air, so empty space costs three checks instead of the full sixteen-variant test. The selector carries gamemode=!spectator,gamemode=!creative, and all three counters are reset at the top of every scan.
- **Entrapment** — DETECTOR. Once every 5 s one candidate player (@r, the bounded pattern redstone/sorting_facility already uses for its 16x7x16 scan) has a 16x4x16 volume around them counted for minecraft:tripwire[attached=true] and minecraft:tripwire_hook[attached=true] - attached=true is what 'armed' means, and it is the same state BACAP redstone/tripping_over keys on. It grants at 32 armed wire blocks and 16 armed hooks, i.e. eight complete hook-to-hook runs of at least four blocks each. Geometry is not checked: eight armed runs that are not literally parallel still count, which is why the description says 'inside a single room' rather than 'parallel'.
- **Full Interchange** — DETECTOR 5 of 5. The bucket_slow gate is four `if block ~ ~ ~` tests against the four rail IDs at the player's own feet -- you have to be standing on a rail, and at most one of the four can match, so the scan runs at most once. It then walks the 9x9 square at that Y level and sets one flag per rail type found; all four flags grant. Deliberately flat (one Y level), because 'one nine-by-nine square' is a layout, not a volume; a junction built across two levels will not count.
- **Golem on Rails** — The one detection function in this batch: no trigger fires when a mob boards a vehicle, so the criteria set is just the injected minecraft:impossible and the grant comes from bacaphd:detect/golem_on_rails. A bucket_medium line runs it as, and positioned at, every non-spectator who lacks the advancement and has a Minecart within 16 blocks; the function then looks for a Minecart within 16 blocks whose Passengers list contains a minecraft:copper_golem — NBT list matching is containment matching, the same shape this pack already uses for the Iron Golem's HandItems poppy — and, positioned at that minecart, tests the block underneath it against the four rail IDs written out one per line, exactly as detect/gc_z does, so no unattested #minecraft:rails tag can fail to resolve. The result is parked on a fake-player score and read back in the player's own context, because after `as @e` the executor is no longer the player. Adjacent to this pack's mining/company_car (a Copper Golem riding a Boat beside you), but that one is the player riding with the golem; here the golem rides alone, on track, and the entry sits in the minecart branch of the redstone tab rather than the copper branch of mining.
- **Hoist by Your Own Petard** — Unchanged this round - no residual defect was reported against it. entity_killed_player fires on the player's death; killing_blow matches an explosion whose direct entity is a primed minecraft:tnt (same shape as BACAP end/dimension_penetration and monsters/keep_your_distance). Ownership is checked: the trigger's `entity` field is damageSource.getEntity(), which for a TNT explosion is PrimedTnt.getOwner() - the player who lit it, propagated down chain reactions and null for redstone- or fire-lit TNT. The loot context that `entity` is matched against uses the dying player's position as ORIGIN, so distance.absolute.max 0.5 means 'the igniter is standing exactly where the victim is', i.e. the igniter IS the victim. That is the same distance trick BACAP uses in reverse in mining/diamonds_to_you (min 1 to exclude self). Another player's trap, a creeper chain and a desert-temple trap all fail it.
- **Jump the Tracks** — DETECTOR 3. bucket_fast, per tick: as a non-creative player whose vehicle is a minecart, positioned at the cart itself (execute ... on vehicle ... at @s on passengers, the pattern the pack already uses for Copper Stable). While a rail is under the cart the take-off point is kept up to date; the first tick without one raises an airborne flag; on landing back on any of the four rails it squares the horizontal displacement and grants at 100 or more (a ten-block hole puts eleven blocks between the take-off and landing rails, so an honest jump clears the threshold even with the one-block truncation error from data get). Rails are listed explicitly rather than via #minecraft:rails, which is not attested in the reference packs.
- **Mass Production** — DETECTOR 2 of 3. Every five seconds an ungranted non-spectator runs a fixed nine-block line scan anchored on the block they are STANDING ON, which must be the first Crafter: Crafter, Hopper, Crafter, Hopper, Crafter, Hopper, Crafter, Hopper, Crafter, tested in all four horizontal directions (four chained executes, no region scan, no recursion). The pattern is palindromic so standing on either end of the line works. `if items block ~2 ~ ~ container.* *` on the second Crafter is the liveness test: it only passes once material has actually travelled through the first Hopper into the next machine, so a decorative row of blocks does not count, and because the scan repeats every five seconds a chain that is momentarily empty between crafts still passes on a later sample. Stated plainly: block facing is NOT verified. A Crafter's orientation is a compound blockstate and a strict facing test would reject perfectly good builds, so the check is geometry plus proven contents rather than a proof of redstone direction.
- **Piston Elevator** — Unchanged this round. Every 10 ticks, if the block under the player's feet is a Slime Block, Honey Block, Piston or Sticky Piston, any gain in altitude since the last sample is added to a running total; stepping off any of those blocks resets the total to zero, so the hundred blocks are continuous. That covers slime flying machines and honey/slime lift shafts alike. The bucket line carries gamemode=!spectator,gamemode=!creative. bacaphd_pe_rise is zeroed on the grant path.
- **Slime Ladder** — REWORKED so a bounce has to be a bounce. The old debounce counted any contact-then-clear cycle, and an ordinary jump from a slime block lifts the feet more than one block, so the block at ~ ~-1 ~ became air, the tag was dropped with OnGround 0 (chain preserved) and the landing counted a 'bounce' -- five standing jumps on one slime block finished it. Now the apex between contacts is measured. detect/slime_bounce fires only on the contact tick (the bucket selector carries tag=!bacaphd.on_slime, so the per-tick cost for a player already standing on slime is one block test and nothing else), latches the contact Y in bacaphd_slime_y and seeds bacaphd_slime_peak with it. While the tag is absent and a chain is live, detect/slime_air maxes bacaphd_slime_peak against the current Y. The next contact runs detect/slime_hop, which requires peak - landing Y >= 30 decimetres: a plain jump peaks about 1.25 blocks up and fails, a real slime rebound (the block reflects almost the whole fall velocity) clears it easily. A failed hop drops the chain to 0 and the current contact becomes the new first contact. detect/slime_air also zeroes the chain if OnGround reads 1 while the feet block is not slime -- that is the 'touching another block' reset. The grant path clears the chain, the apex and the tag, and creative is excluded because creative flight removes the bouncing entirely.
- **Slow Burn** — Function-granted. The player stands on the run; bucket_slow anchors on the aligned block under their feet, and only if that block is a `repeater[delay=4]` does it walk. detect/slow_burn takes one step with `^ ^ ^1` and recurses, so the caller picks the direction purely by setting `rotated`: X is walked both ways, the count is tested, then it is reset to the anchor and Z is walked both ways, so the two axes can never be added together. The walk starts from the centre of the anchor block, which makes each `^ ^ ^1` an exact one-block step with no float drift off the line. Any repeater met along the way that reads powered=true sets a flag, and the grant needs sixteen contiguous four-tick repeaters plus that flag. The one softness I will name: the powered flag is not cleared between the X and the Z pass, so a powered stub on the other axis would satisfy it — it still has to be part of the same contiguous build.
- **Sorting Facility** — Unchanged this round. A bounded 16x7x16 block scan centred on the player, run on the slow bucket for at most one candidate player per cycle (selected with @r so the cost is capped no matter how many people lack it), counts Hoppers and Chests and grants when both reach 27. The selector carries gamemode=!spectator,gamemode=!creative, since this measures block placement. It checks quantity in a chunk-sized footprint, not that each Hopper actually feeds a Chest - no command can trace redstone or item flow, and the description does not claim otherwise. Both counters are reset at the top of every scan.
- **Tickling the Ivories** — bucket_slow picks at most ONE candidate player per cycle with @r - the same cost cap HD already uses for Sorting Facility, Clock Tower, Copper Counter and Monochrome - and detect/tickling_the_ivories reads the run from the block the player is standing ON, following it east, west, south and north at once. Four flags start at 1 and any block that is not minecraft:note_block[note=N] at offset N clears the flag for its own direction only, so a keyboard laid out in any of the four directions passes and nothing else does. 100 bounded `if block` tests, no world writes, no entity scans. The description tells the player where to stand because the anchor has to be a known block; the check cannot search for the start of the run without a region sweep.

</details>
